      <!-- SCREEN: TRAINING SIMULATIONS -->
      <section id="screen-cbt-mock-hub" class="screen">
        <h1>MISSION SIMULATOR <span style="font-size:0.6rem; font-weight:700; background:#f59e0b; color:#000; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:99px; letter-spacing:0.1em; vertical-align:middle; position:relative; top:-4px;">BETA</span></h1>
        <p class="subtitle">Prepare in real Computer-Based Test (CBT) environment using official marking patterns and timers</p>

        <div id="cbt-view-exams" class="full-page-view active">
          <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(200px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; padding: 20px 0;">
            <div class="subject-card" onclick="openCbtExam('NDA')" style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; text-align: center; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s ease;">
              <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">🛡️</div>
              <h3 style="margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 1.1rem; color: #ef4444;">NDA</h3>
              <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted);">National Defence Academy</div>
            </div>
            <div class="subject-card" onclick="openCbtExam('CDS')" style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(135, 169, 107, 0.3); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; text-align: center; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s ease;">
              <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">⚔️</div>
              <h3 style="margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 1.1rem; color: #87a96b;">CDS</h3>
              <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted);">Combined Defence Services</div>
            </div>
            <div class="subject-card" onclick="openCbtExam('AFCAT')" style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; text-align: center; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s ease;">
              <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">✈️</div>
              <h3 style="margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 1.1rem; color: #38bdf8;">AFCAT</h3>
              <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted);">Air Force Common Admission</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="cbt-view-list" class="full-page-view" style="display: none;">
          <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px; gap: 16px;">
            <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="backToCbtExams()" style="padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 6px;">
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 16px; height: 16px; display: inline-block; vertical-align: middle; margin-right: 4px;">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9 15L3 9m0 0l6-6M3 9h12a6 6 0 010 12h-3" />
              </svg>
              Back to Exams
            </button>
            <h2 id="cbt-active-exam-title" style="margin: 0; flex: 1;"></h2>
          </div>

          <!-- PYQ Semantic Search Widget -->
          <div class="panel glow" style="margin-bottom: 24px; border-left: 4px solid var(--accent); background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.2) 0%, rgba(22, 163, 74, 0.05) 100%);">
            <div class="panel-title" style="margin-bottom: 8px;">PYQ Semantic Search Engine</div>
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 16px;">
              Instantly search across our entire database of Mock Exams and Past Year Questions. Find specific topics like "Ocean Currents" or "Integration" to practice targeted questions.
            </p>
            <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
              <input type="text" id="pyq-search-input" placeholder="e.g., Fundamental Rights, Matrices, River systems..." style="flex: 1; min-width: 200px; padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.95rem; outline: none;" onkeydown="if(event.key === 'Enter') executePyqSearch()">
              <button class="ai-btn" onclick="executePyqSearch()" style="width: auto; padding: 0 24px;">
                <span>Search PYQs</span>
                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 18px; height: 18px;">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m21 21-5.197-5.197m0 0A7.5 7.5 0 1 0 5.196 5.196a7.5 7.5 0 0 0 10.602 10.602Z" />
                </svg>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div id="pyq-search-results" style="margin-top: 16px; display: none;">
              <!-- Rendered PYQ Flashcards -->
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div class="exams-grid" id="exams-list-container">
            <!-- CBT Exam Mock cards built dynamically -->
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- SCREEN: ASK DRONACHARYA -->
      <section id="screen-ai-console" class="screen">
        <h1>Ask Dronacharya</h1>
        <p class="subtitle">Generate targeted MCQ question sets or get comprehensive topic explanations powered by Dronacharya AI — completely free</p>
        
        <div class="ai-console">
          <div class="panel">
            <div class="panel-title">Enter Concept / Topic</div>
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 0.9rem;">
              Type any topic or concept name. Select <strong>Generate Questions</strong> for an MCQ set with solutions, <strong>Solve Doubt</strong> for a comprehensive AI-generated explanation, or <strong>Visual Reconnaissance</strong> to upload an image and be quizzed on it. Both modes are <span style="color: var(--accent); font-weight: 600;">completely free</span> — no tokens deducted.
            </p>
            
            <div class="ai-suggestions" id="ai-suggestion-container">
              <!-- Preset topic bubbles -->
            </div>
            
            <div class="ai-query-box">
  <div class="ai-query-input-row">
    <input type="text" class="ai-input" id="ai-custom-topic-input" placeholder="e.g., Matrices and Determinants, Fundamental Rights Articles, Newton's Laws of Motion...">
    <button class="ai-btn" id="ai-generate-btn">
      <span>Run</span>
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 18px; height: 18px;">
        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m3.75 13.5 10.5-11.25L12 10.5h8.25L9.75 21.75 12 13.5H3.75Z" />
      </svg>
    </button>
  </div>
  <div class="ai-mode-selector">
    <label><input type="radio" name="ai-mode" value="questions" checked> Generate Questions</label>
    <label><input type="radio" name="ai-mode" value="solve"> Solve Doubt</label>
    <label><input type="radio" name="ai-mode" value="recon"> Visual Reconnaissance Quiz <span style="font-size:0.6rem; font-weight:bold; background:var(--accent); color:#000; padding:1px 4px; border-radius:3px;">NEW</span></label>
    <label><input type="radio" name="ai-mode" value="cheatsheet"> Generate Cheat Sheet</label>
  </div>
  <div class="ai-image-upload-row" id="ai-image-upload-container" style="display: none; margin-top: 12px; align-items: center; gap: 12px; border: 1px dashed var(--border); padding: 12px; border-radius: 8px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.1);">
    <label style="font-size: 0.85rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-secondary); white-space: nowrap; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;">
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 18px; height: 18px; color: var(--accent);">
        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m2.25 15.75 5.159-5.159a2.25 2.25 0 0 1 3.182 0l5.159 5.159m-1.5-1.5 1.409-1.409a2.25 2.25 0 0 1 3.182 0l2.909 2.909m-18 3.75h16.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 1.5-1.5V6a1.5 1.5 0 0 0-1.5-1.5H3.75A1.5 1.5 0 0 0 2.25 6v12a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 1.5 1.5Zm10.5-11.25h.008v.008h-.008V8.25Zm.375 0a.375 0 1 1-.75 0 .375 0 0 1 .75 0Z" />
      </svg>
      Attach Doubt Image (JPG, JPEG, PNG):
    </label>
    <input type="file" id="ai-doubt-image-input" accept="image/png, image/jpeg, image/jpg" style="padding: 6px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer; flex: 1; font-size: 0.85rem;">
  </div>
</div>
          </div>
          
          <!-- AI response layout -->
          <div class="ai-response-area" id="ai-result-area">
            <!-- Dynamic compiled questionnaire -->
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      

      <!-- SCREEN: AI PAPER SOLVER -->
      <section id="screen-paper-solver" class="screen">
        <h1>AI Paper Solver & Evaluator</h1>
        <p class="subtitle">Solve past exam papers or evaluate your completed OMR/answer sheets using Gemini AI.</p>

        <!-- Mode Toggle Switch -->
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; margin-top: 16px; margin-bottom: 24px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); padding-bottom: 12px;">
          <button class="exam-btn active" id="btn-mode-solve-paper" onclick="togglePaperSolverMode('solve')">Solve Question Paper</button>
          <button class="exam-btn" id="btn-mode-evaluate-omr" onclick="togglePaperSolverMode('evaluate')">Evaluate Answer Sheet (OMR)</button>
        </div>

        <!-- Mode 1: Solve Question Paper -->
        <div id="panel-solve-paper-container">
          <div class="panel">
            <div class="panel-title">Upload Exam Paper (PDF)</div>
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 0.9rem;">
              Upload your PDF file. The AI will extract the questions and generate the answers. Note: For very large exams (120+ questions), the AI may only solve a portion per run due to output length limits.
            </p>

            <!-- Range Configuration Options -->
            <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; margin-bottom: 20px; max-width: 500px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02); padding: 16px; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border);">
              <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px;">
                <label style="font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.5px;">SOLVE PAGE RANGE</label>
                <select id="ai-paper-page-preset" style="padding: 8px; border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.85rem;" onchange="toggleCustomPageInput()">
                  <option value="all">Solve All Pages (Max Questions)</option>
                  <option value="1-5" selected>Pages 1 to 5 (Questions ~1-20)</option>
                  <option value="6-10">Pages 6 to 10 (Questions ~20-40)</option>
                  <option value="11-15">Pages 11 to 15 (Questions ~40-60)</option>
                  <option value="16-20">Pages 16 to 20 (Questions ~60-80)</option>
                  <option value="21-25">Pages 21 to 25 (Questions ~80-100)</option>
                  <option value="26-30">Pages 26 to 30 (Questions ~100-120)</option>
                  <option value="custom">Custom Page Range...</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div id="ai-paper-custom-range-container" style="display: none; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px;">
                <label style="font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.5px;">CUSTOM PAGES</label>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center; height: 100%;">
                  <input type="number" id="ai-paper-start-page" placeholder="Start" min="1" value="1" style="width: 75px; padding: 8px; border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.85rem;" />
                  <span style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.85rem;">to</span>
                  <input type="number" id="ai-paper-end-page" placeholder="End" min="1" value="5" style="width: 75px; padding: 8px; border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.85rem;" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Checkbox for Fast Answer Key Only -->
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
              <input type="checkbox" id="ai-paper-no-explanation" style="width: 18px; height: 18px; cursor: pointer; accent-color: var(--accent);" checked />
              <label for="ai-paper-no-explanation" style="font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-secondary); cursor: pointer; user-select: none; font-weight: 500;">
                Fast Answer Key Only (No explanations, prevents token limit cut-offs)
              </label>
            </div>

            <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 24px;">
              <input type="file" id="ai-paper-upload" accept="application/pdf" style="padding: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer; width: 100%; max-width: 400px;" />
              <button class="ai-btn" id="ai-solve-paper-btn" style="white-space: nowrap;">
                <span>Solve Paper</span>
                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 18px; height: 18px;">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="m3.75 13.5 10.5-11.25L12 10.5h8.25L9.75 21.75 12 13.5H3.75Z" />
                </svg>
              </button>
            </div>

            <div class="ai-response-area" id="paper-solver-result-area" style="display: none; margin-top: 20px;">
              <!-- Rendered output -->
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Mode 2: Evaluate Answer Sheet (OMR) -->
        <div id="panel-evaluate-omr-container" style="display: none;">
          <div class="panel">
            <div class="panel-title">Evaluate Answer Sheet (OMR / Marked Pages)</div>
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 0.9rem;">
              Upload a PDF or image scan of your filled OMR sheet, OR a scanned handwritten list of answers (e.g. 1-a, 2-b) on paper. You can also type them manually below. Specify the exam and subject, and let Gemini AI score your attempt automatically.
            </p>

            <!-- Exam metadata inputs -->
            <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; margin-bottom: 20px; max-width: 600px;">
              <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px;">
                <label style="font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.5px;">TARGET EXAM NAME</label>
                <input type="text" id="ai-eval-exam-name" placeholder="e.g., CDS 1 2026, NDA 2 2025, AFCAT 1 2026" style="padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.85rem; font-family: var(--font-main); outline: none;" />
              </div>
              <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px;">
                <label style="font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.5px;">SUBJECT / PAPER TYPE</label>
                <select id="ai-eval-subject" style="padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.85rem; font-family: var(--font-main); outline: none; cursor: pointer;">
                  <option value="maths">Mathematics</option>
                  <option value="gs-gat">General Studies / GAT</option>
                  <option value="english">English</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- File Upload and Action -->
            <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 12px;">
              <input type="file" id="ai-omr-upload" accept="application/pdf,image/*" style="padding: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer; width: 100%; max-width: 400px;" />
            </div>
            
            <div style="margin-bottom: 20px; max-width: 600px;">
              <label style="font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.5px; display: block; margin-bottom: 6px;">OR TYPE YOUR ANSWERS HERE (e.g. 1. a, 2. c...)</label>
              <textarea id="ai-omr-text" rows="3" placeholder="If you don't have a scanned OMR image, just type your answers here..." style="width: 100%; padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-tertiary); border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--text-primary); font-size: 0.9rem; font-family: var(--font-mono); outline: none; resize: vertical;"></textarea>
            </div>

            <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 20px;">
              <button class="ai-btn" id="ai-evaluate-omr-btn" style="white-space: nowrap;">
                <span>Evaluate Answer Sheet</span>
                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 18px; height: 18px;">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9 12.75 11.25 15 15 9.75M21 12c0 1.268-.63 2.39-1.593 3.068a3.745 3.745 0 0 1-1.043 3.296 3.745 3.745 0 0 1-3.296 1.043A3.745 3.745 0 0 1 12 21c-1.268 0-2.39-.63-3.068-1.593a3.746 3.746 0 0 1-3.296-1.043 3.745 3.745 0 0 1-1.043-3.296A3.745 3.745 0 0 1 3 12c0-1.268.63-2.39 1.593-3.068a3.745 3.745 0 0 1 1.043-3.296 3.746 3.746 0 0 1 3.296-1.043A3.746 3.746 0 0 1 12 3c1.268 0 2.39.63 3.068 1.593a3.746 3.746 0 0 1 3.296 1.043 3.746 3.746 0 0 1 1.043 3.296A3.745 3.745 0 0 1 21 12Z" />
                </svg>
              </button>
            </div>

            <div class="ai-response-area" id="omr-evaluation-result-area" style="display: none; margin-top: 20px;">
              <!-- Rendered output -->
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- SCREEN: VOCAB BUILDER -->
      <section id="screen-vocab-builder" class="screen">
        <h1>English Vocab Builder</h1>
        <p class="subtitle">Master high-frequency synonyms and antonyms for NDA, CDS, and AFCAT</p>
        
        <div id="vocab-view-modes" class="full-page-view active">
          <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(200px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; padding: 20px 0;">
            <div class="subject-card" onclick="openVocabMode('word')" style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border: 1px solid var(--border); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; text-align: center; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s ease;">
              <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">📖</div>
              <h3 style="margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 1.1rem;">Word of the Day</h3>
              <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted);">Random Vocabulary</div>
            </div>
            <div class="subject-card" onclick="openVocabMode('quiz')" style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border: 1px solid var(--border); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; text-align: center; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s ease;">
              <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">🧠</div>
              <h3 style="margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 1.1rem;">Vocab Drill</h3>
              <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted);">5-Question Practice</div>
            </div>
            <div class="subject-card" onclick="openVocabMode('dictionary')" style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border: 1px solid var(--border); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; text-align: center; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s ease;">
              <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">🔍</div>
              <h3 style="margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 1.1rem;">AI Dictionary</h3>
              <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted);">Search words & idioms</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="vocab-view-word" class="full-page-view" style="display: none;">
          <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="backToVocabModes()" style="padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 6px;">
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 16px; height: 16px; display: inline-block; vertical-align: middle; margin-right: 4px;">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9 15L3 9m0 0l6-6M3 9h12a6 6 0 010 12h-3" />
              </svg>
              Back
            </button>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Word of the Day / Random Word Panel -->
          <div class="panel glow" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--accent); display: flex; flex-direction: column; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto;">
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 16px;">
              <div class="panel-title" style="margin-bottom: 0;">Word of the Day</div>
              <span id="vocab-date-label" style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted); background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 4px;"></span>
            </div>
            
            <div id="vocab-card" style="text-align: center; padding: 24px 16px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05); flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center;">
              <h2 id="vocab-word" style="font-size: 2.5rem; color: var(--text-primary); margin-bottom: 8px; font-family: var(--font-logo); letter-spacing: 1px;">Loading...</h2>
              <div style="font-size: 1rem; color: var(--accent); margin-bottom: 16px; font-weight: 600;" id="vocab-pos"></div>
              
              <div style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; margin-bottom: 16px;">
                <div style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.85rem; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin-bottom: 4px;">Meaning</div>
                <div id="vocab-meaning" style="font-size: 1.1rem; color: var(--text-primary);"></div>
              </div>
              
              <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px; text-align: left;">
                <div style="background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.05); padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.1);">
                  <div style="color: var(--info); font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px;">SYNONYMS</div>
                  <div id="vocab-synonyms" style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem;"></div>
                </div>
                <div style="background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.05); padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1);">
                  <div style="color: var(--danger); font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px;">ANTONYMS</div>
                  <div id="vocab-antonyms" style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem;"></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <button class="btn-primary" onclick="loadRandomWord()" style="margin-top: 16px; width: 100%;">Get Another Random Word</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="vocab-view-quiz" class="full-page-view" style="display: none;">
          <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="backToVocabModes()" style="padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 6px;">
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 16px; height: 16px; display: inline-block; vertical-align: middle; margin-right: 4px;">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9 15L3 9m0 0l6-6M3 9h12a6 6 0 010 12h-3" />
              </svg>
              Back
            </button>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Daily Vocab Quiz Panel -->
          <div class="panel" style="max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto;">
            <div class="panel-title">5-Question Vocab Drill</div>
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 20px;">
              Test your knowledge on synonyms and antonyms. Generate a new drill anytime.
            </p>
            
            <div id="vocab-quiz-container">
              <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px;">
                <button class="btn-primary" onclick="startVocabQuiz()" style="width: auto; padding: 12px 32px; font-size: 1.1rem;">Start Daily Drill</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="vocab-view-dictionary" class="full-page-view" style="display: none;">
          <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="backToVocabModes()" style="padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 6px;">
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="2" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 16px; height: 16px; display: inline-block; vertical-align: middle; margin-right: 4px;">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9 15L3 9m0 0l6-6M3 9h12a6 6 0 010 12h-3" />
              </svg>
              Back
            </button>
          </div>
          
          <div class="panel glow" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--accent); display: flex; flex-direction: column; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto;">
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 16px;">
              <div class="panel-title" style="margin-bottom: 0;">AI Dictionary</div>
            </div>
            
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 20px;">
              Search for the exact meaning, synonyms, and antonyms of any word or idiom using AI.
            </p>
            
            <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 24px;">
              <input type="text" id="dict-search-input" placeholder="Enter a word or idiom..." style="flex: 1; padding: 12px 16px; border-radius: 6px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2); color: #fff; font-size: 1.1rem; outline: none;" onkeypress="if(event.key === 'Enter') searchDictionary()">
              <button class="btn-primary" onclick="searchDictionary()" style="padding: 12px 24px; width: auto; flex-shrink: 0; font-size: 1.1rem;">Search</button>
            </div>
            
            <div id="dict-loading" style="display: none; text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: var(--text-muted);">
              <div class="spinner" style="border-top-color: var(--accent); width: 30px; height: 30px; margin: 0 auto 16px;"></div>
              <div>Consulting AI...</div>
            </div>
            
            <div id="dict-results" style="display: none; text-align: center; padding: 24px 16px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05); flex: 1; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center;">
              <h2 id="dict-word" style="font-size: 2.2rem; color: var(--text-primary); margin-bottom: 8px; font-family: var(--font-logo); letter-spacing: 1px;">Word</h2>
              <div style="font-size: 1rem; color: var(--accent); margin-bottom: 16px; font-weight: 600;" id="dict-pos"></div>
              
              <div style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; margin-bottom: 16px; text-align: left;">
                <div style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.85rem; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin-bottom: 4px;">Meaning</div>
                <div id="dict-meaning" style="font-size: 1.1rem; color: var(--text-primary);"></div>
              </div>
              
              <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px; text-align: left;">
                <div style="background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.05); padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.1);">
                  <div style="color: var(--info); font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px;">SYNONYMS</div>
                  <div id="dict-synonyms" style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem;"></div>
                </div>
                <div style="background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.05); padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1);">
                  <div style="color: var(--danger); font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px;">ANTONYMS</div>
                  <div id="dict-antonyms" style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem;"></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div id="dict-error" style="display: none; text-align: center; padding: 20px; color: var(--danger); background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1); border-radius: 6px; margin-top: 16px;"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section id="screen-advanced-solver" class="screen">
        <div class="content-wrapper">
          <div class="dashboard-header" style="margin-bottom: 24px;">
            <h1 class="glow-text">Advanced Math Solver</h1>
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); max-width: 600px;">
              Powered by Google Search Grounding. Paste any math question below. The AI will search the internet for the exact solution, and fall back to calculating it step-by-step.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div class="glass-panel" style="padding: 24px; margin-bottom: 24px;">
            <textarea id="advanced-solver-input" class="search-input" placeholder="Type or paste your math question here..." style="width: 100%; min-height: 150px; padding: 16px; margin-bottom: 16px; border-radius: 8px; font-family: var(--font-main); resize: vertical;"></textarea>
            <button class="btn-primary" onclick="solveAdvancedMath()" id="advanced-solver-btn" style="width: 100%; padding: 12px; font-size: 1.1rem; display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
              <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 10V3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z" />
              </svg>
              Search & Solve
            </button>
          </div>

          <div class="glass-panel" id="advanced-solver-result-container" style="display: none; padding: 24px;">
            <h3 style="color: var(--accent); margin-bottom: 16px; font-family: var(--font-logo);">SOLUTION</h3>
            <div id="advanced-solver-result" class="markdown-body" style="background: transparent; color: var(--text);"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>



    
      <!-- SCREEN: EXTRA QUESTION BANK -->
      <section id="screen-question-bank" class="screen">
        <div class="screen-header">
            <h1>Question Armoury</h1>
            <p class="subtitle">Browse thousands of unused PYQs segregated by subject and chapter.</p>
        </div>
        
        <div id="armoury-breadcrumb" class="breadcrumb" style="margin-bottom: 20px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; color: var(--accent);">
            <!-- Breadcrumbs injected via JS -->
        </div>

        <div id="armoury-navigation" class="grid-layout" style="margin-bottom: 30px;">
            <!-- Hierarchy cards (Domain -> Subject -> Chapter) injected via JS -->
        </div>

        <div id="bank-container" class="bank-container" style="display: none;">
            <!-- Cards injected via JS -->
        </div>
        
        <div class="load-more-container" style="text-align: center; padding: 20px;">
            <button id="load-more-btn" class="start-btn" onclick="loadMoreBankQuestions()" style="display: none;">Load More Questions</button>
        </div>
      </section>



      <!-- ONBOARDING PAYMENT SCREEN -->
      <section id="screen-onboarding-payment" class="screen">
        <div class="content-header">
          <h2>Account Activation</h2>
          <p>Complete your payment of ₹99 to unlock the application forever.</p>
          <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="signOutAndGoToLanding()" style="margin-top: 10px; padding: 6px 12px; font-size: 0.85rem;">Sign Out & View Features</button>
        </div>
        <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns: 1fr; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; text-align: center;">
          <div class="card" style="padding: 2rem;">
            <h3 style="margin-bottom: 1rem;">Scan QR to Pay</h3>
            <img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=200x200&data=upi%3A%2F%2Fpay%3Fpa%3Dtrayodh%40okicici%26pn%3DJayastra%26am%3D99.00%26cu%3DINR%26tn%3DJayastra%2520Premium" alt="Payment QR Code" style="width: 250px; height: 250px; border-radius: 12px; margin: 0 auto 1.5rem auto; display: block; background: #fff; padding: 10px;">
            
            <div style="margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
              <a href="upi://pay?pa=trayodh@okicici&pn=Jayastra&am=99.00&cu=INR&tn=Jayastra%20Premium" style="color: var(--accent); text-decoration: underline; font-size: 1.1rem; font-weight: bold; font-family: var(--font-main);">Tap here to pay via UPI App</a>
            </div>

            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">After making the payment, please enter your Transaction ID below for manual verification.</p>
            
            <div style="text-align: left; margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
              <label style="display: block; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Transaction ID / UTR Number</label>
              <input type="text" id="onboarding-transaction-id" placeholder="e.g. 123456789012" style="width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid var(--border); background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2); color: var(--text-primary);">
            </div>
            
            <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="submitOnboardingPayment()" style="width: 100%; justify-content: center;">Submit Proof</button>
            <p id="onboarding-payment-msg" style="margin-top: 1rem; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--accent); display: none;">Payment submitted! Your account is currently locked pending manual verification.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- LOCKED SCREEN -->
      <section id="screen-locked" class="screen">
        <div class="content-header">
          <h2>Account Locked</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns: 1fr; max-width: 500px; margin: 0 auto; text-align: center;">
          <div class="card" style="padding: 3rem 2rem;">
            <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5" stroke="currentColor" style="width: 64px; height: 64px; margin: 0 auto 1.5rem auto; color: var(--accent);">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M16.5 10.5V6.75a4.5 4.5 0 1 0-9 0v3.75m-.75 11.25h10.5a2.25 2.25 0 0 0 2.25-2.25v-6.75a2.25 2.25 0 0 0-2.25-2.25H6.75a2.25 2.25 0 0 0-2.25 2.25v6.75a2.25 2.25 0 0 0 2.25 2.25Z" />
            </svg>
            <h3 style="margin-bottom: 1rem;">Access Revoked by Admin</h3>
            <p style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Your access to the platform has been restricted. Please contact support if you believe this is an error.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- ADMIN DASHBOARD SCREEN -->
      <section id="screen-admin" class="screen">
        <div class="content-header">
          <h2>Admin Dashboard</h2>
          <p>Manage user access and payment approvals.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="card" style="overflow-x: auto;">
          <table style="width: 100%; text-align: left; border-collapse: collapse;">
            <thead>
              <tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);">
                <th style="padding: 12px; color: var(--text-secondary); white-space: nowrap;">Email</th>
                <th style="padding: 12px; color: var(--text-secondary); white-space: nowrap;">Status</th>
                <th style="padding: 12px; color: var(--text-secondary); white-space: nowrap;">Transaction ID</th>
                <th style="padding: 12px; color: var(--text-secondary); white-space: nowrap;">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody id="admin-users-list">
              <!-- Rendered via JS -->
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

    </main>
  </div>

  <!-- FULL SCREEN CBT LAYER PLAYER -->
  <div class="cbt-overlay" id="cbt-player-overlay" style="display: none;">
    <div class="cbt-header">
      <div class="cbt-exam-title" id="cbt-active-title">NDA MATHEMATICS MOCK PAPER 1</div>
      <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-left: auto; margin-right: 24px;">
        <span style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--accent); font-weight: bold; letter-spacing: 0.5px;">AUDIO COCKPIT HUD</span>
        <label class="switch">
          <input type="checkbox" id="cbt-audio-hud-toggle" onchange="toggleAudioHud(this.checked)">
          <span class="slider round"></span>
        </label>
      </div>
      <div class="cbt-timer" id="cbt-active-timer">00:00:00</div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cbt-main">
      <!-- Left side: Question and options -->
      <div class="cbt-question-pane">
        <div>
          <div class="cbt-question-header">
            <span id="cbt-question-index-label">Question 1 of 100</span>
            <span id="cbt-marking-scheme-label">Correct: +2.5 | Incorrect: -0.83</span>
          </div>
          <div class="cbt-question-text" id="cbt-active-question-text">
            What is the integration of cot(x) dx?
          </div>
          <div class="cbt-options-list" id="cbt-options-container">
            <!-- Options A, B, C, D -->
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <!-- Controls for navigation inside pane -->
        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-top: 24px; gap: 12px; width: 100%;">
          <button class="action-btn" id="cbt-btn-prev">← Previous</button>
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px;">
            <button class="action-btn" id="cbt-btn-clear">Clear Responsesnse</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="cbt-btn-mark-review"> Mark for Review</button>
            <button class="action-btn" id="cbt-btn-scratchpad" onclick="toggleScratchpad()">Scratchpad</button>
          </div>
          <button class="btn-primary" id="cbt-btn-save-next" style="width: auto; padding-left: 24px; padding-right: 24px;">Save & Next →️</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- Right side: Palette and Legend -->
      <aside class="cbt-control-pane">
        <div>
          <!-- Legend -->
          <div class="legend-section">
            <div class="legend-item">
              <div class="legend-dot answered"></div>
              <span>Answered</span>
            </div>
            <div class="legend-item">
              <div class="legend-dot not-answered"></div>
              <span>Not Answered</span>
            </div>
            <div class="legend-item">
              <div class="legend-dot marked-review"></div>
              <span>Marked for Review</span>
            </div>
            <div class="legend-item">
              <div class="legend-dot not-visited"></div>
              <span>Not Visited</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Palette Grid -->
          <div class="palette-section">
            <div class="palette-title">Question Palette</div>
            <div class="palette-grid" id="cbt-palette-grid-container">
              <!-- Rendered palette boxes -->
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <!-- Side control buttons -->
        <div style="padding: 20px; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px;">
          <button class="btn-warning" id="cbt-btn-submit-exam">End Test</button>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>

    <!-- Floating Canvas Scratchpad -->
    <div id="cbt-scratchpad-container" style="display: none; position: absolute; top: 120px; right: 20px; width: 90vw; max-width: 420px; height: 380px; background: rgba(10, 15, 10, 0.95); border: 2px solid var(--accent); border-radius: 8px; z-index: 100; box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(132, 204, 22, 0.4); flex-direction: column;">
      <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; padding: 10px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); background: rgba(0,0,0,0.6);">
        <span style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--accent); font-weight: bold; letter-spacing: 0.5px;">TACTICAL SCRATCHPAD</span>
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center;">
          <span style="font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-secondary);">Size:</span>
          <select id="scratchpad-size" style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); color: white; border: 1px solid var(--border); font-size: 0.7rem; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer; padding: 2px;">
            <option value="2">2px</option>
            <option value="4" selected>4px</option>
            <option value="6">6px</option>
          </select>
          <button onclick="clearScratchpad()" style="padding: 3px 10px; background: var(--danger); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 0.75rem; cursor: pointer; font-weight: bold;">Clear</button>
          <button onclick="toggleScratchpad()" style="padding: 3px 10px; background: #475569; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 0.75rem; cursor: pointer; font-weight: bold;">Close</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <canvas id="cbt-scratchpad-canvas" style="flex: 1; cursor: crosshair; background: transparent;"></canvas>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- CBT REPORT OVERLAY VIEW -->
  <div class="cbt-overlay" id="cbt-report-overlay" style="display: none; overflow-y: auto;">
    <div class="cbt-header">
      <div class="cbt-exam-title">CBT EXAM PERFORMANCE SHEET</div>
      <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="closeCbtReport()">Back to Command Centre</button>
    </div>
    
    <div style="padding: 32px 16px; max-width: 900px; margin: 0 auto; width: 100%;">
      <div class="panel result-card">
        <div class="result-header">
          <h2 id="report-exam-title">NDA Mathematics Mock Paper 1</h2>
          <div class="result-score-container">
            <span class="result-score-value" id="report-final-score">82.5</span>
            <span class="result-score-label">Net Score</span>
          </div>
          <p id="report-verdict" style="color: var(--accent); font-weight: 600; margin-top: 8px;">QUALIFIED (Cut-off surpassed)</p>
        </div>
        
        <table class="result-table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Metric</th>
              <th>Value</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr>
              <td>Total Questions</td>
              <td id="report-total-qs">120</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>Attempted</td>
              <td id="report-attempted-qs">90</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>Correct Answers</td>
              <td id="report-correct-qs">75 (Gain +187.5)</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>Incorrect Answers</td>
              <td id="report-incorrect-qs">15 (Loss -12.45)</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>Accuracy</td>
              <td id="report-accuracy">83.3%</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
        
        <!-- Interactive Question Grid Heatmap -->
        <div class="heatmap-section" style="margin-top: 24px; padding: 16px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06); border-radius: 8px;">
          <h3 style="margin-bottom: 12px; font-family: var(--font-logo); font-size: 0.95rem; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.5px;">TACTICAL QUESTION PERFORMANCE HEATMAP</h3>
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; font-size: 0.72rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 12px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;"><span style="width: 12px; height: 12px; background: #22c55e; border-radius: 2px; display: inline-block;"></span> Correct</div>
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;"><span style="width: 12px; height: 12px; background: #ef4444; border-radius: 2px; display: inline-block;"></span> Incorrect</div>
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;"><span style="width: 12px; height: 12px; background: #4b5563; border-radius: 2px; display: inline-block;"></span> Unattempted</div>
          </div>
          <div id="report-heatmap-grid" style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(28px, 1fr)); gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 12px;">
            <!-- Rendered squares -->
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Radar Chart Analytics (Micro-Topic Breakdown) -->
        <div class="radar-analytics-section" style="margin-top: 24px; padding: 16px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06); border-radius: 8px;">
          <h3 style="margin-bottom: 12px; font-family: var(--font-logo); font-size: 0.95rem; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.5px;">MICRO-TOPIC RADAR ANALYTICS</h3>
          <p style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 16px;">This spider chart visualizes your performance across different sub-topics tested in this mission simulator. Use this to identify exactly which chapter you need to revise next.</p>
          <div style="position: relative; height: 350px; width: 100%; display: flex; justify-content: center;">
            <canvas id="report-radar-chart"></canvas>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- AI Corrections & Weakness Insight -->
        <div id="report-ai-reconciliation" style="margin-top: 24px; padding: 16px; background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2); border-radius: 8px;">
          <h3 style="margin: 0 0 10px 0; font-family: var(--font-logo); font-size: 0.95rem; color: var(--accent); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
            <div class="cbt-spinner" id="report-ai-spinner" style="border-color: var(--accent); border-top-color: transparent; width: 14px; height: 14px; border-width: 2px; display: inline-block; margin-right: 6px;"></div>
            🤖 DRONACHARYA AI ERROR RECONCILIATION
          </h3>
          <div id="report-ai-analysis-text" style="font-size: 0.88rem; line-height: 1.6; color: var(--text-secondary);">
            Analyzing wrong answers and formulating corrective actions...
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="solution-review" style="margin-top: 32px;">
          <h3 style="margin-bottom: 20px; font-family: var(--font-logo);">Corrections & Solutions Explanation</h3>
          <div id="report-solutions-list">
            <!-- Render solutions dynamically with explanations -->
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <!-- Ask Dronacharya is now the FAB panel at the end of body -->

  <!-- THEME SELECTOR MODAL -->
  <div class="cbt-overlay" id="theme-selector-modal" style="display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center; z-index: 10000; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.8);">
    <div class="panel" style="max-width: 400px; width: 100%; position: relative;">
      <button class="chatbot-close-btn" onclick="document.getElementById('theme-selector-modal').style.display='none'" style="position: absolute; top: 12px; right: 12px;">&times;</button>
      <h2 style="font-family: var(--font-logo); font-size: 1.2rem; margin-bottom: 20px; text-align: center;"> Select Theme</h2>
      
      <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px;">
        <button class="exam-btn" onclick="applyTheme('default')" style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; text-align: left;">
          <span>Command Center (Tactical Blue)</span>
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 4px;">
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #04080f; border: 1px solid #00e5ff; box-shadow: 0 0 4px #00e5ff;"></div>
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #00e5ff; border: 1px solid #04080f;"></div>
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #0277bd; border: 1px solid #04080f;"></div>
          </div>
        </button>

        <button class="exam-btn" onclick="applyTheme('vajra')" style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; text-align: left;">
          <span>Vajra (Red/Black/Gold)</span>
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 4px;">
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #000000; border: 1px solid #ef4444; box-shadow: 0 0 4px #ef4444;"></div>
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #ef4444; border: 1px solid #000000;"></div>
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #ffd700; border: 1px solid #000000;"></div>
          </div>

        <button class="exam-btn" onclick="applyTheme('woodland-camo')" style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; text-align: left;">
          <span>Jungle Commando (Army)</span>
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 4px;">

            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #39ff14; border: 1px solid #020a04;"></div>
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #00ced1; border: 1px solid #020a04;"></div>
          </div>
        </button>

        <button class="exam-btn" onclick="applyTheme('desert-camo')" style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; text-align: left;">
          <span>Desert Storm (Infantry)</span>
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 4px;">
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #0d0a06; border: 1px solid #ffaa00; box-shadow: 0 0 4px #ffaa00;"></div>
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #ffaa00; border: 1px solid #0d0a06;"></div>
            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #ff6f00; border: 1px solid #0d0a06;"></div>

        <button class="exam-btn" onclick="applyTheme('urban-camo')" style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; text-align: left;">

            <div style="width: 16px; height: 16px; border-radius: 50%; background: #ffffff; border: 1px solid #060606;"></div>

        <button class="exam-btn" onclick="applyTheme('desert-storm')" style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; text-align: left;">

  <!-- SRS REVIEW MODAL -->
  <div class="cbt-overlay" id="srs-review-modal" style="display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center; z-index: 9999; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.85);">
    <div class="panel" style="max-width: 500px; width: 100%; position: relative; padding: 30px;">
      <button class="chatbot-close-btn" onclick="document.getElementById('srs-review-modal').style.display='none'" style="position: absolute; top: 12px; right: 12px;">&times;</button>
      
      <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">
        <h2 style="font-family: var(--font-logo); font-size: 1.3rem; margin: 0; color: var(--warning);">Daily Briefing</h2>
        <span id="srs-progress" style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-muted); background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 4px;">1 / 5</span>
      </div>

      <div style="margin-bottom: 24px;">
        <div id="srs-topic-label" style="font-size: 0.8rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--info); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin-bottom: 8px;">TOPIC NAME</div>
        <div id="srs-question-text" style="font-size: 1.1rem; color: var(--text-primary); line-height: 1.5;">Question text goes here?</div>
      </div>

      <div id="srs-answer-container" style="display: none; margin-bottom: 24px; padding-top: 20px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--border);">
        <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--success); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin-bottom: 8px;">ANSWER / KEY CONCEPT</div>
        <div id="srs-answer-text" style="font-size: 1rem; color: var(--text-secondary); line-height: 1.5;">Answer explanation goes here.</div>
      </div>

      <div id="srs-controls-reveal" style="text-align: center;">
        <button class="btn-primary" onclick="revealSrsAnswer()" style="width: 100%; padding: 12px; font-size: 1.05rem;">Reveal Answer</button>
      </div>

      <div id="srs-controls-grade" style="display: none; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px;">
        <p style="text-align: center; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 4px;">How well did you remember this?</p>
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px;">
          <button id="srs-btn-again" class="action-btn" onclick="gradeSrsItem(1)" style="flex: 1; border-color: var(--danger); color: var(--danger);">Again (1)</button>
          <button id="srs-btn-hard" class="action-btn" onclick="gradeSrsItem(3)" style="flex: 1; border-color: var(--warning); color: var(--warning);">Hard (3)</button>
          <button id="srs-btn-good" class="action-btn" onclick="gradeSrsItem(4)" style="flex: 1; border-color: var(--info); color: var(--info);">Good (4)</button>
          <button id="srs-btn-easy" class="action-btn" onclick="gradeSrsItem(5)" style="flex: 1; border-color: var(--success); color: var(--success);">Easy (5)</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>


  <!-- ONBOARDING MODAL (BRANCH SELECTION) -->
  <div class="cbt-overlay" id="onboarding-modal" style="display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center;">
    <div class="panel" style="max-width: 500px; width: 100%; padding: 32px; text-align: center;">
      <h2 style="margin-bottom: 8px; color: var(--success);">Welcome to Tac-Revise</h2>
      <p style="margin-bottom: 32px; color: var(--text-secondary);">Select your preferred service branch. Your ranks will be based on this choice.</p>
      
      <div style="display: flex; gap: 16px; justify-content: center; margin-bottom: 32px;">
        <div class="branch-card" onclick="selectBranch('army')" id="branch-card-army" style="flex: 1; padding: 24px 16px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">
          <div style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">⚔️</div>
          <div style="font-weight: 600;">Indian Army</div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="branch-card" onclick="selectBranch('navy')" id="branch-card-navy" style="flex: 1; padding: 24px 16px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">
          <div style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">⚓</div>
          <div style="font-weight: 600;">Indian Navy</div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="branch-card" onclick="selectBranch('airforce')" id="branch-card-airforce" style="flex: 1; padding: 24px 16px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">
          <div style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 12px;">✈️</div>
          <div style="font-weight: 600;">Air Force</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button class="btn-primary" style="width: 100%; padding: 12px;" onclick="confirmBranchSelection()">Confirm Selection</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- SPACED REPETITION (SRS) REVIEW MODAL -->
  <div class="cbt-overlay" id="srs-review-modal" style="display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center; z-index: 1000;">
    <div class="panel srs-modal-panel" style="max-width: 600px; width: 100%; padding: 28px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 20px; border: 2px solid var(--warning); box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25);">
      <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
          <span style="font-size: 1.5rem;">🧠</span>
          <h2 style="margin: 0; color: var(--warning); font-family: var(--font-logo); font-size: 1.3rem; letter-spacing: 0.5px;">TACTICAL MEMORY DECK</h2>
        </div>
        <span id="srs-progress" style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 4px;">0 / 0</span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="srs-card" style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px; padding: 20px; min-height: 180px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; position: relative;">
        <span id="srs-topic-label" style="position: absolute; top: 12px; left: 16px; font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.65rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--warning); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1); border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2); padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 3px;">TOPIC</span>
        <div id="srs-question-text" style="font-size: 1.05rem; line-height: 1.6; color: var(--text-primary); text-align: center; margin-top: 15px;">Question Text</div>
        
        <div id="srs-answer-container" style="display: none; margin-top: 20px; padding-top: 20px; border-top: 1px dashed rgba(255,255,255,0.1); text-align: center;">
          <div style="font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.65rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--success); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin-bottom: 8px;">Memory Key</div>
          <div id="srs-answer-text" style="font-size: 0.95rem; line-height: 1.55; color: var(--text-secondary);">Answer Text</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- Reveal controls -->
      <div id="srs-controls-reveal" style="text-align: center;">
        <button class="btn-primary" onclick="revealSrsAnswer()" style="background: var(--warning); border-color: var(--warning); padding: 12px 36px; font-weight: 600; width: 100%;">REVEAL MEMORY KEY</button>
      </div>
      
      <!-- Grading controls (hidden initially) -->
      <div id="srs-controls-grade" style="display: none; gap: 8px; justify-content: space-between;">
        <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="gradeSrsItem(1)" style="flex: 1; border-color: #ef4444; color: #ef4444; background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08); padding: 10px 0;">🔴 Forgot</button>
        <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="gradeSrsItem(3)" style="flex: 1; border-color: #f59e0b; color: #f59e0b; background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.08); padding: 10px 0;">🟡 Hard</button>
        <button class="btn-secondary" onclick="gradeSrsItem(5)" style="flex: 1; border-color: #10b981; color: #10b981; background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.08); padding: 10px 0;">🟢 Easy</button>
      </div>
      
      <div style="text-align: center;">
        <span style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted); cursor: pointer; text-decoration: underline;" onclick="document.getElementById('srs-review-modal').style.display='none'">Abort Mission (Close)</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Mobile Sidebar Toggle Logic -->
  <script>
  (function() {
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('mobile-menu-toggle');
    const sidebar   = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const overlay   = document.getElementById('sidebar-overlay');
    if (!toggleBtn || !sidebar || !overlay) return;

    // Right chevron (closed state: "open me")
    const arrowRightSVG = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="3" stroke="currentColor" width="18" height="18"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M8.25 4.5l7.5 7.5-7.5 7.5"/></svg>';
    // Left chevron (open state: "close me")
    const arrowLeftSVG  = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="3" stroke="currentColor" width="18" height="18"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M15.75 19.5L8.25 12l7.5-7.5"/></svg>';

    function openMenu() {
      sidebar.classList.add('open');
      overlay.classList.add('visible');
      toggleBtn.classList.add('open');
      toggleBtn.innerHTML = arrowLeftSVG;
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }

    function closeMenu() {
      sidebar.classList.remove('open');
      overlay.classList.remove('visible');
      toggleBtn.classList.remove('open');
      toggleBtn.innerHTML = arrowRightSVG;
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }

    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (sidebar.classList.contains('open')) {
        closeMenu();
      } else {
        openMenu();
      }
    });

    overlay.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);

    // Auto-close menu when a nav item is tapped
    sidebar.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(item) {
      item.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
          closeMenu();
        }
      });
    });

    // Close menu on resize to desktop
    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
      if (window.innerWidth > 768) {
        sidebar.classList.remove('open');
        overlay.classList.remove('visible');
        toggleBtn.classList.remove('open');
        toggleBtn.innerHTML = arrowRightSVG;
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
      }
    });
  })();
  </script>
